It then performs Multi-Agent Collaborative Reasoning: a Router Agent dynamically dispatches Specialist Agents based on case complexity; these agents iteratively reason over the evidence and trigger targeted re-retrievals when needed, while a Generalist Agent synthesizes all deliberations into a traceable consensus diagnosis that emulates multidisciplinary consultation. Experimental results on hepatic disease cases from MIMIC-IV show that MedCoRAG outperforms existing methods and closed-source models in both diagnostic performance and reasoning interpretability.

</details>


### [152] [KARL: Knowledge Agents via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05218)
*Jonathan D. Chang,Andrew Drozdov,Shubham Toshniwal,Owen Oertell,Alexander Trott,Jacob Portes,Abhay Gupta,Pallavi Koppol,Ashutosh Baheti,Sean Kulinski,Ivan Zhou,Irene Dea,Krista Opsahl-Ong,Simon Favreau-Lessard,Sean Owen,Jose Javier Gonzalez Ortiz,Arnav Singhvi,Xabi Andrade,Cindy Wang,Kartik Sreenivasan,Sam Havens,Jialu Liu,Peyton DeNiro,Wen Sun,Michael Bendersky,Jonathan Frankle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出KARL系统，通过强化学习训练企业搜索代理，在多样化搜索任务上达到SOTA性能，并引入KARLBench评估套件和创新的数据合成与训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业搜索任务多样化且难以验证，现有模型在异构搜索行为上的泛化能力不足，需要开发能够处理多种搜索场景的高性能知识代理系统。

Method: 1) 引入KARLBench六种搜索能力评估套件；2) 开发代理合成管道生成多样化高质量训练数据；3) 提出基于迭代大批量离策略RL的后训练范式，支持多任务训练和OOD泛化。

Result: KARL在KARLBench上对Claude 4.6和GPT 5.2实现帕累托最优，在成本-质量和延迟-质量权衡中表现优异，包括训练时未见过的OOD任务，在足够测试计算下超越最强闭源模型。

Conclusion: 定制的合成数据结合多任务强化学习能够实现成本高效且高性能的知识代理，用于基于事实的推理任务，为企业搜索代理训练提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We present a system for training enterprise search agents via reinforcement learning that achieves state-of-the-art performance across a diverse suite of hard-to-verify agentic search tasks. Our work makes four core contributions. First, we introduce KARLBench, a multi-capability evaluation suite spanning six distinct search regimes, including constraint-driven entity search, cross-document report synthesis, tabular numerical reasoning, exhaustive entity retrieval, procedural reasoning over technical documentation, and fact aggregation over internal enterprise notes. Second, we show that models trained across heterogeneous search behaviors generalize substantially better than those optimized for any single benchmark. Third, we develop an agentic synthesis pipeline that employs long-horizon reasoning and tool use to generate diverse, grounded, and high-quality training data, with iterative bootstrapping from increasingly capable models. Fourth, we propose a new post-training paradigm based on iterative large-batch off-policy RL that is sample efficient, robust to train-inference engine discrepancies, and naturally extends to multi-task training with out-of-distribution generalization. Compared to Claude 4.6 and GPT 5.2, KARL is Pareto-optimal on KARLBench across cost-quality and latency-quality trade-offs, including tasks that were out-of-distribution during training. With sufficient test-time compute, it surpasses the strongest closed models. These results show that tailored synthetic data in combination with multi-task reinforcement learning enables cost-efficient and high-performing knowledge agents for grounded reasoning.

</details>


### [153] [AI+HW 2035: Shaping the Next Decade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05225)
*Deming Chen,Jason Cong,Azalia Mirhoseini,Christos Kozyrakis,Subhasish Mitra,Jinjun Xiong,Cliff Young,Anima Anandkumar,Michael Littman,Aron Kirschen,Sophia Shao,Serge Leef,Naresh Shanbhag,Dejan Milojicic,Michael Schulte,Gert Cauwenberghs,Jerry M. Chow,Tri Dao,Kailash Gopalakrishnan,Richard Ho,Hoshik Kim,Kunle Olukotun,David Z. Pan,Mark Ren,Dan Roth,Aarti Singh,Yizhou Sun,Yusu Wang,Yann LeCun,Ruchir Puri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AI与硬件协同设计的10年路线图，旨在通过跨层优化实现1000倍能效提升，构建可持续、自适应、覆盖云边端的智能系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI与硬件发展轨迹紧密交织但缺乏协调的长期愿景，碎片化限制了构建可持续、自适应、高效AI系统的进展。未来AI发展不仅需要扩展智能，更需要提升能效（每焦耳智能），而非无限制的计算消耗。

Method: 提出10年AI+HW协同设计与开发路线图，涵盖算法、架构、系统和可持续性。强调围绕能效、系统级集成和跨层优化的重新定义扩展，结合算法创新、硬件进步和软件抽象的综合解决方案。

Result: 定义了10年成功的具体目标：实现AI训练和推理1000倍能效提升；支持跨云、边缘和物理AI的无缝能源感知自优化系统；民主化先进AI基础设施访问；将人本原则嵌入智能系统设计。

Conclusion: 呼吁学术界、产业界、政府和社区采取具体行动，包括协调的国家倡议、共享基础设施、人才培养、跨机构合作和持续的公私伙伴关系，使AI+HW协同设计成为统一的长期使命。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) and hardware (HW) are advancing at unprecedented rates, yet their trajectories have become inseparably intertwined. The global research community lacks a cohesive, long-term vision to strategically coordinate the development of AI and HW. This fragmentation constrains progress toward holistic, sustainable, and adaptive AI systems capable of learning, reasoning, and operating efficiently across cloud, edge, and physical environments. The future of AI depends not only on scaling intelligence, but on scaling efficiency, achieving exponential gains in intelligence per joule, rather than unbounded compute consumption. Addressing this grand challenge requires rethinking the entire computing stack. This vision paper lays out a 10-year roadmap for AI+HW co-design and co-development, spanning algorithms, architectures, systems, and sustainability. We articulate key insights that redefine scaling around energy efficiency, system-level integration, and cross-layer optimization. We identify key challenges and opportunities, candidly assess potential obstacles and pitfalls, and propose integrated solutions grounded in algorithmic innovation, hardware advances, and software abstraction. Looking ahead, we define what success means in 10 years: achieving a 1000x improvement in efficiency for AI training and inference; enabling energy-aware, self-optimizing systems that seamlessly span cloud, edge, and physical AI; democratizing access to advanced AI infrastructure; and embedding human-centric principles into the design of intelligent systems. Finally, we outline concrete action items for academia, industry, government, and the broader community, calling for coordinated national initiatives, shared infrastructure, workforce development, cross-agency collaboration, and sustained public-private partnerships to ensure that AI+HW co-design becomes a unifying long-term mission.

</details>


### [154] [Reclaiming Lost Text Layers for Source-Free Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05235)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Guangyao Chen,Yixiong Zou,Yuhua Li,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现CLIP文本编码器的某些中间层在跨域少样本学习中看似冗余，但实际包含有用信息，只是视觉差距阻碍了其充分利用。作者提出重新利用这些"丢失层"信息的方法，在层和编码器层面指导视觉分支在域偏移下的重新学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SF-CDFSL研究中发现CLIP的文本编码器更适合跨域任务，但作者观察到移除文本编码器的某些中间层反而能提升性能。这引发了对这一现象的深入探究：这些层是否真的有害？其中的信息是否被浪费了？

Method: 提出重新利用"丢失层"信息的方法，在层级和编码器层面指导视觉分支的学习。通过重新利用这些层中的信息，帮助模型在域偏移下更好地重新学习视觉表示，而不是简单地移除这些层。

Result: 在多种设置、骨干网络（CLIP、SigLip、PE-Core）和任务（4个CDFSL数据集和10个Meta-dataset数据集）上的广泛实验证明了方法的有效性，有效解决了文本编码器中信息利用不足的问题。

Conclusion: 文本编码器的某些中间层在SF-CDFSL中并非有害，而是包含有用信息，只是视觉差距阻碍了其充分利用。通过重新利用这些层的信息，可以显著提升跨域少样本学习的性能。

Abstract: Source-Free Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning (SF-CDFSL) focuses on fine-tuning with limited training data from target domains (e.g., medical or satellite images), where CLIP has recently shown promising results due to its generalizability to downstream tasks. Current works indicate CLIP's text encoder is more suitable for cross-domain tasks, however, we find that \textbf{removing certain middle layers of the text encoder can effectively improve performance in SF-CDFSL}, which we call the Lost Layers. In this paper, we delve into this phenomenon for a deeper understanding. We discover that instead of being harmful for the SF-CDFSL task, the information in these layers is actually beneficial, but visual gaps prevent this useful information from being fully utilized, making these layers seem redundant. Based on this understanding, unlike current works that simply remove these layers, we propose a method to teachs the model to \textbf{re-utilize} information in these lost layers at both the layer and encoder levels, guiding the re-learning of the visual branch under domain shifts. Our approach effectively addresses the issue of underutilized information in the text encoder. Extensive experiments across various settings, backbones (CLIP, SigLip, PE-Core), and tasks (4 CDFSL datasets and 10 Meta-dataset datasets) demonstrate the effectiveness of our method. Code is available at https://github.com/zhenyuZ-HUST/CVPR26-VtT.

</details>


### [155] [GCAgent: Enhancing Group Chat Communication through Dialogue Agents System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05240)
*Zijie Meng,Zheyong Xie,Zheyu Ye,Chonggang Lu,Zuozhu Liu,Zihan Niu,Yao Hu,Shaosheng Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GCAgent是一个基于大语言模型的系统，用于增强群聊沟通，通过娱乐和实用导向的对话代理提升群聊活跃度和参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线社交平台中的群聊是兴趣交流和问题解决的重要形式，但其效果常因不活跃和管理挑战而受限。虽然大语言模型已赋能一对一对话代理，但在多参与者对话中的无缝集成尚未探索。

Method: 系统包含三个紧密集成的模块：Agent Builder根据用户兴趣定制代理；Dialogue Manager协调对话状态和管理代理调用；Interface Plugins通过三种不同工具降低交互障碍。

Result: 实验显示GCAgent在各项标准上平均得分4.68，51.04%的情况下优于基础模型。在350天的实际部署中，消息量增加了28.80%，显著提升了群聊活跃度和参与度。

Conclusion: 这项工作为将基于大语言模型的对话代理从单方聊天扩展到多方群聊场景提供了实用蓝图。

Abstract: As a key form in online social platforms, group chat is a popular space for interest exchange or problem-solving, but its effectiveness is often hindered by inactivity and management challenges. While recent large language models (LLMs) have powered impressive one-to-one conversational agents, their seamlessly integration into multi-participant conversations remains unexplored. To address this gap, we introduce GCAgent, an LLM-driven system for enhancing group chats communication with both entertainment- and utility-oriented dialogue agents. The system comprises three tightly integrated modules: Agent Builder, which customizes agents to align with users' interests; Dialogue Manager, which coordinates dialogue states and manage agent invocations; and Interface Plugins, which reduce interaction barriers by three distinct tools. Through extensive experiment, GCAgent achieved an average score of 4.68 across various criteria and was preferred in 51.04\% of cases compared to its base model. Additionally, in real-world deployments over 350 days, it increased message volume by 28.80\%, significantly improving group activity and engagement. Overall, this work presents a practical blueprint for extending LLM-based dialogue agent from one-party chats to multi-party group scenarios.

</details>


### [156] [X-RAY: Mapping LLM Reasoning Capability via Formalized and Calibrated Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05290)
*Gao Tianxi,Cai Yufan,Yuan Yusi,Dong Jin Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: X-RAY系统通过形式化校准的探针评估LLM的推理能力，发现LLM在约束细化时相对稳健，但在解空间重构时表现急剧下降


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要关注任务级准确率，往往混淆模式匹配与推理能力，需要更精确的方法来理解和评估LLM的推理能力

Method: 提出X-RAY系统，通过形式化工具生成具有受控结构变化的探针，使用形式化校准和验证来精确分离增量结构信息，将推理能力建模为可提取结构的函数

Result: 发现LLM推理存在系统性不对称：对约束细化相对稳健，但在解空间重构时表现急剧下降；校准的形式探针能区分标准基准上看似无差异的模型，并揭示结构可解释而非不透明的失败模式

Conclusion: X-RAY框架提供了一种无污染、可形式化验证的评估方法，不仅能评估LLM推理能力，还能支持推理模型的训练和测试，揭示了LLM推理能力的结构性局限

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve promising performance, yet their ability to reason remains poorly understood. Existing evaluations largely emphasize task-level accuracy, often conflating pattern matching with reasoning capability. We present X-RAY, an explainable reasoning analysis system that maps the LLM reasoning capability using calibrated, formally verified probes. We model reasoning capability as a function of extractable \textit{structure}, operationalized through formal properties such as constraint interaction, reasoning depth, and solution-space geometry. X-Ray generates probes via formal tools with controlled structural variations, enabling precise isolation of incremental structural information through formal calibration and verification. We evaluate state-of-the-art LLMs on problems ranging from junior-level to advanced in mathematics, physics, and chemistry. Our analysis reveals a systematic asymmetry in LLM reasoning: models are relatively robust to constraint refinement, where additional conditions shrink an existing solution space, but degrade sharply under solution-space restructuring, where modifications alter the underlying structural form of the solution manifold. Moreover, calibrated formal probes differentiate models that appear indistinguishable on standard benchmarks and reveal failure modes that are structurally interpretable rather than opaque. Beyond evaluation, our framework is contamination-free and supports the training and testing of reasoning models.

</details>


### [157] [STRUCTUREDAGENT: Planning with AND/OR Trees for Long-Horizon Web Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05294)
*ELita Lobo,Xu Chen,Jingjing Meng,Nan Xi,Yang Jiao,Chirag Agarwal,Yair Zick,Yan Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STRUCTUREDAGENT是一个用于长时程网页浏览任务的分层规划框架，通过动态AND/OR树进行高效搜索，结合结构化记忆模块提升约束满足能力，相比标准LLM智能体表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页智能体在复杂长时程任务上表现不佳，主要受限于上下文记忆容量不足、规划能力弱以及贪婪行为导致过早终止。需要解决这些挑战以提升智能体在网页浏览任务中的表现。

Method: 提出STRUCTUREDAGENT框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 使用动态AND/OR树进行高效搜索的在线分层规划器；2) 跟踪和维护候选解决方案的结构化记忆模块，提升信息搜索任务中的约束满足能力。

Result: 在WebVoyager、WebArena和自定义购物基准测试中，STRUCTUREDAGENT相比标准基于LLM的智能体，在长时程网页浏览任务上表现更优。

Conclusion: STRUCTUREDAGENT通过分层规划和结构化记忆有效解决了现有网页智能体的局限性，不仅提升了性能，还提供了可解释的分层计划，便于调试和人工干预。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled agentic systems for sequential decision-making. Such agents must perceive their environment, reason across multiple time steps, and take actions that optimize long-term objectives. However, existing web agents struggle on complex, long-horizon tasks due to limited in-context memory for tracking history, weak planning abilities, and greedy behaviors that lead to premature termination. To address these challenges, we propose STRUCTUREDAGENT, a hierarchical planning framework with two core components: (1) an online hierarchical planner that uses dynamic AND/OR trees for efficient search and (2) a structured memory module that tracks and maintains candidate solutions to improve constraint satisfaction in information-seeking tasks. The framework also produces interpretable hierarchical plans, enabling easier debugging and facilitating human intervention when needed. Our results on WebVoyager, WebArena, and custom shopping benchmarks show that STRUCTUREDAGENT improves performance on long-horizon web-browsing tasks compared to standard LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [158] [WebChain: A Large-Scale Human-Annotated Dataset of Real-World Web Interaction Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05295)
*Sicheng Fan,Rui Wan,Yifei Leng,Gaoning Liang,Li Ling,Yanyi Shang,Dehan Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WebChain是最大的开源真实网站人标注轨迹数据集，包含31,725条轨迹和318k步骤，提出双重中期训练方法在WebChainBench上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了加速可复现的网页智能体研究，需要大规模、高质量的真实网站交互数据集，现有合成方法往往无法覆盖复杂高价值任务。

Method: 1) 构建WebChain数据集：通过可扩展流水线收集视觉、结构和动作数据的三重对齐多模态监督数据；2) 提出双重中期训练方法：将空间定位与规划解耦。

Result: WebChain包含31,725条轨迹和318k步骤，是最大的开源真实网站人标注轨迹数据集。提出的双重中期训练方法在WebChainBench和其他公共GUI基准测试中达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: WebChain为构建和严格评估下一代可扩展网页智能体提供了必要的数据和洞见，推动了网页智能体研究的可复现性。

Abstract: We introduce WebChain, the largest open-source dataset of human-annotated trajectories on real-world websites, designed to accelerate reproducible research in web agents. It contains 31,725 trajectories and 318k steps, featuring a core Triple Alignment of visual, structural, and action data to provide rich, multi-modal supervision. The data is collected via a scalable pipeline that ensures coverage of complex, high-value tasks often missed by synthetic methods. Leveraging this dataset, we propose a Dual Mid-Training recipe that decouples spatial grounding from planning, achieving state-of-the-art performance on our proposed WebChainBench and other public GUI benchmarks. Our work provides the data and insights necessary to build and rigorously evaluate the next generation of scalable web agents.

</details>


### [159] [UniSTOK: Uniform Inductive Spatio-Temporal Kriging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05301)
*Lewei Xie,Haoyu Zhang,Juan Yuan,Liangjun You,Yulong Chen,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UniSTOK是一个即插即用的时空克里金框架，通过双分支输入和掩码调制处理传感器数据中的异构缺失问题，提升现有模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中传感器数据存在高度异构的缺失问题，传统方法依赖粗略插值，面临三个挑战：无法区分真实信号与缺失伪影、缺失模式高度异构、缺失破坏局部时空结构。

Method: 提出双分支输入架构：原始观测分支和拼图增强分支（在缺失位置合成代理信号），通过共享时空骨干网络处理，使用显式缺失掩码调制，最后通过双通道注意力自适应融合输出。

Result: 在多种真实世界数据集和不同缺失模式下的实验表明，该方法能带来一致且显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: UniSTOK框架能有效处理传感器数据中的异构缺失问题，增强现有时空克里金模型的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal kriging aims to infer signals at unobserved locations from observed sensors and is critical to applications such as transportation and environmental monitoring. In practice, however, observed sensors themselves often exhibit heterogeneous missingness, forcing inductive kriging models to rely on crudely imputed inputs. This setting brings three key challenges: (1) it is unclear whether an value is a true signal or a missingness-induced artifact; (2) missingness is highly heterogeneous across sensors and time; (3) missing observations distort the local spatio-temporal structure. To address these issues, we propose Uniform Inductive Spatio-Temporal Kriging (UniSTOK), a plug-and-play framework that enhances existing inductive kriging backbones under missing observation. Our framework forms a dual-branch input consisting of the original observations and a jigsaw-augmented counterpart that synthesizes proxy signals only at missing entries. The two branches are then processed in parallel by a shared spatio-temporal backbone with explicit missingness mask modulation. Their outputs are finally adaptively fused via dual-channel attention. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets under diverse missing patterns demonstrate consistent and significant improvements.

</details>


### [160] [Building AI Coding Agents for the Terminal: Scaffolding, Harness, Context Engineering, and Lessons Learned](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05344)
*Nghi D. Q. Bui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OPENDEV是一个开源的命令行AI编码助手，采用终端原生架构，通过复合AI系统、双智能体设计、惰性工具发现和自适应上下文压缩等技术，解决长时程开发任务中的安全控制和上下文管理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码辅助正从复杂的IDE插件转向终端原生智能体，但长时程开发任务需要严格的安全控制和高效的上下文管理，以防止上下文膨胀和推理能力下降。

Method: 采用复合AI系统架构，包括：1）工作负载专用模型路由；2）规划与执行分离的双智能体架构；3）惰性工具发现；4）渐进减少旧观察的自适应上下文压缩；5）跨会话积累项目知识的自动化记忆系统；6）事件驱动的系统提醒机制。

Result: OPENDEV提供了一个安全、可扩展的终端优先AI辅助基础，为稳健的自主软件工程提供了蓝图。

Conclusion: 通过强制显式推理阶段和优先考虑上下文效率，OPENDEV为终端原生AI编码助手建立了新的范式，解决了长时程开发任务中的关键挑战。

Abstract: The landscape of AI coding assistance is undergoing a fundamental shift from complex IDE plugins to versatile, terminal-native agents. Operating directly where developers manage source control, execute builds, and deploy environments, CLI-based agents offer unprecedented autonomy for long-horizon development tasks. In this paper, we present OPENDEV, an open-source, command-line coding agent engineered specifically for this new paradigm. Effective autonomous assistance requires strict safety controls and highly efficient context management to prevent context bloat and reasoning degradation. OPENDEV overcomes these challenges through a compound AI system architecture with workload-specialized model routing, a dual-agent architecture separating planning from execution, lazy tool discovery, and adaptive context compaction that progressively reduces older observations. Furthermore, it employs an automated memory system to accumulate project-specific knowledge across sessions and counteracts instruction fade-out through event-driven system reminders. By enforcing explicit reasoning phases and prioritizing context efficiency, OPENDEV provides a secure, extensible foundation for terminal-first AI assistance, offering a blueprint for robust autonomous software engineering.

</details>


### [161] [Ailed: A Psyche-Driven Chess Engine with Dynamic Emotional Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05352)
*Diego Armando Resendez Prado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出人格-心理分解框架，通过音频信号链动态调整棋步概率分布，模拟人类棋手的行为变化，无需额外搜索或状态保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统象棋引擎虽然超越人类水平，但缺乏人类棋手的行为变异性（如压力下的失误、过度自信等）。本文旨在通过人格-心理分解来模拟人类棋手的行为模式，使引擎表现更接近人类。

Method: 采用人格（静态预设）和心理（动态标量ψ_t）分解。心理值基于五个位置因素每步更新，通过音频信号链（噪声门、压缩器/扩展器、五段均衡器、饱和限制器）实时重塑棋步概率分布。该框架独立于底层引擎，适用于任何输出概率的系统。

Result: 在12,414局对Maia2-1100的测试中，两种训练数据量相差2800倍的概率源都显示出相同的行为变化模式：过度自信时与原始引擎一致率达66%，压力下竞争得分从50.8%降至30.1%。行为变化主要来自信号链而非底层模型。

Conclusion: 人格-心理分解框架成功模拟了人类棋手的行为变异性（如倾斜和过度自信），但缺乏人类主体验证。该信号链方法通用性强，可应用于任何概率输出系统，为AI行为模拟提供了新思路。

Abstract: Chess engines passed human strength years ago, but they still don't play like humans. A grandmaster under clock pressure blunders in ways a club player on a hot streak never would. Conventional engines capture none of this.
  This paper proposes a personality x psyche decomposition to produce behavioral variability in chess play, drawing on patterns observed in human games. Personality is static -- a preset that pins down the engine's character. Psyche is dynamic -- a bounded scalar ψ_t \in [-100, +100], recomputed from five positional factors after every move. These two components feed into an audio-inspired signal chain (noise gate, compressor/expander, five-band equalizer, saturation limiter) that reshapes move probability distributions on the fly. The chain doesn't care what engine sits behind it: any system that outputs move probabilities will do. It needs no search and carries no state beyond ψ_t.
  I test the framework across 12,414 games against Maia2-1100, feeding it two probability sources that differ by ~2,800x in training data. Both show the same monotonic gradient in top-move agreement (~20-25 pp spread from stress to overconfidence), which tells us the behavioral variation comes from the signal chain, not from the model underneath. When the psyche runs overconfident, the chain mostly gets out of the way (66% agreement with vanilla Maia2). Under stress, the competitive score falls from 50.8% to 30.1%. The patterns are reminiscent of tilt and overconfidence as described in human play, but I should be upfront: this study includes no human-subject validation.

</details>


### [162] [PACE: A Personalized Adaptive Curriculum Engine for 9-1-1 Call-taker Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05361)
*Zirong Chen,Hongchao Zhang,Meiyi Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE是一个用于911呼叫接听培训的个性化自适应课程引擎，通过概率技能状态建模、学习遗忘动态分析和智能场景推荐，显著提升培训效率和效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 911呼叫接听培训涉及上千项相互依赖的技能，需要个性化教学，但全国性劳动力短缺和培训师无法规模化个性化指导的现实问题亟待解决。

Method: PACE系统通过：(1)维护学员技能状态的概率信念，(2)建模个体学习和遗忘动态，(3)在结构化技能图上传播证据以加速诊断覆盖，(4)使用上下文多臂老虎机选择针对学员准备填补技能缺口的训练场景。

Result: PACE相比最先进框架实现19.50%更快的胜任时间，10.95%更高的最终掌握度。与专家教学判断的匹配率达95.45%，评估时间从11.58分钟减少到仅34秒（减少95.08%）。

Conclusion: PACE作为培训师的协同系统，能够有效扩展个性化培训规模，显著提升911呼叫接听培训的效率和质量，解决当前培训体系面临的规模化挑战。

Abstract: 9-1-1 call-taking training requires mastery of over a thousand interdependent skills, covering diverse incident types and protocol-specific nuances. A nationwide labor shortage is already straining training capacity, but effective instruction still demands that trainers tailor objectives to each trainee's evolving competencies. This personalization burden is one that current practice cannot scale. Partnering with Metro Nashville Department of Emergency Communications (MNDEC), we propose PACE (Personalized Adaptive Curriculum Engine), a co-pilot system that augments trainer decision-making by (1) maintaining probabilistic beliefs over trainee skill states, (2) modeling individual learning and forgetting dynamics, and (3) recommending training scenarios that balance acquisition of new competencies with retention of existing ones. PACE propagates evidence over a structured skill graph to accelerate diagnostic coverage and applies contextual bandits to select scenarios that target gaps the trainee is prepared to address. Empirical results show that PACE achieves 19.50% faster time-to-competence and 10.95% higher terminal mastery compared to state-of-the-art frameworks. Co-pilot studies with practicing training officers further demonstrate a 95.45% alignment rate between PACE's and experts' pedagogical judgments on real-world cases. Under estimation, PACE cuts turnaround time to merely 34 seconds from 11.58 minutes, up to 95.08% reduction.

</details>


### [163] [Legal interpretation and AI: from expert systems to argumentation and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05392)
*Václav Janeček,Giovanni Sartor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文综述了AI与法律研究中处理法律解释的三种主要方法：专家系统、论证理论和机器学习，分析了各自在知识工程、论证结构和自动化生成方面的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI在法律领域的应用不断发展，需要系统梳理不同研究方法如何应对法律解释这一核心挑战，为AI与法律研究的未来发展提供方向。

Method: 采用文献综述和分类分析的方法，将AI与法律研究中的法律解释方法分为三大类：专家系统方法（关注知识工程）、论证理论方法（关注论证结构）和机器学习方法（关注自动化生成）。

Result: 识别出三种主要研究范式：1）专家系统通过知识工程确保人类解释的精确转移和一致应用；2）论证理论通过论证框架评估解释主张的可接受性；3）机器学习通过语言模型自动生成解释建议和论证。

Conclusion: AI与法律研究在法律解释方面形成了多元化的方法论路径，从早期的知识工程到当前的机器学习应用，这些方法正在逐步融入法律实践，为法律解释的自动化和智能化提供了不同层面的支持。

Abstract: AI and Law research has encountered legal interpretation in different ways, in the context of its evolving approaches and methodologies. Research on expert system has focused on legal knowledge engineering, with the goal of ensuring that human-generated interpretations can be precisely transferred into knowledge-bases, to be consistently applied. Research on argumentation has aimed at representing the structure of interpretive arguments, as well as their dialectical interactions, to assess of the acceptability of interpretive claims within argumentation frameworks. Research on machine learning has focused on the automated generation of interpretive suggestions and arguments, through general and specialised language models, now being increasingly deployed in legal practice.

</details>


### [164] [Judge Reliability Harness: Stress Testing the Reliability of LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05399)
*Sunishchal Dev,Andrew Sloan,Joshua Kavner,Nicholas Kong,Morgan Sandler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Judge Reliability Harness开源库，用于测试LLM评委的可靠性，发现现有评委在不同基准测试和扰动类型下表现差异显著，没有评委在所有测试中表现一致可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM评分在AI基准测试中广泛部署，需要更多工具来有效评估这些方法的可靠性。现有LLM评委在不同任务和扰动下可能存在一致性问题。

Method: 开发了一个开源库，给定基准数据集和LLM评委配置，生成可靠性测试，评估二元判断准确性和序数分级性能，支持自由回答和代理任务格式。

Result: 评估了四个最先进的评委在四个基准测试（安全性、说服力、滥用、代理行为）上的表现，发现模型和扰动类型之间存在显著性能差异。没有评委在所有基准测试中表现一致可靠，发现了文本格式化、转述、冗长变化和标签翻转等简单变化导致的评委一致性问题。

Conclusion: LLM评委的可靠性需要系统评估，现有评委在不同测试场景下表现不稳定，存在改进空间。该工具为评估和提升LLM评委的鲁棒性提供了重要支持。

Abstract: We present the Judge Reliability Harness, an open source library for constructing validation suites that test the reliability of LLM judges. As LLM based scoring is widely deployed in AI benchmarks, more tooling is needed to efficiently assess the reliability of these methods. Given a benchmark dataset and an LLM judge configuration, the harness generates reliability tests that evaluate both binary judgment accuracy and ordinal grading performance for free-response and agentic task formats. We evaluate four state-of-the-art judges across four benchmarks spanning safety, persuasion, misuse, and agentic behavior, and find meaningful variation in performance across models and perturbation types, highlighting opportunities to improve the robustness of LLM judges. No judge that we evaluated is uniformly reliable across benchmarks using our harness. For example, our preliminary experiments on judges revealed consistency issues as measured by accuracy in judging another LLM's ability to complete a task due to simple text formatting changes, paraphrasing, changes in verbosity, and flipping the ground truth label in LLM-produced responses. The code for this tool is available at: https://github.com/RANDCorporation/judge-reliability-harness

</details>


### [165] [Dissociating Direct Access from Inference in AI Introspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05414)
*Harvey Lederman,Kyle Mahowald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI模型通过概率匹配和直接访问内部状态两种机制进行内省，其中直接访问机制是内容无关的，模型能检测异常但无法可靠识别语义内容


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内省是基础认知能力但其机制尚不清楚，近期研究表明AI模型能够内省，本文旨在研究AI模型内省的机制

Method: 首先广泛复现Lindsey等人(2025)的思想注入检测范式，在大型开源模型中研究内省机制，分析概率匹配和直接访问内部状态两种机制

Result: 模型通过两种可分离机制检测注入表征：概率匹配(从感知到的提示异常推断)和直接访问内部状态；直接访问机制是内容无关的，模型能检测异常但无法可靠识别语义内容；模型会虚构高频具体概念，正确概念猜测通常需要更多token

Conclusion: 这种内容无关的内省机制与哲学和心理学中的主要理论相一致

Abstract: Introspection is a foundational cognitive ability, but its mechanism is not well understood. Recent work has shown that AI models can introspect. We study their mechanism of introspection, first extensively replicating Lindsey et al. (2025)'s thought injection detection paradigm in large open-source models. We show that these models detect injected representations via two separable mechanisms: (i) probability-matching (inferring from perceived anomaly of the prompt) and (ii) direct access to internal states. The direct access mechanism is content-agnostic: models detect that an anomaly occurred but cannot reliably identify its semantic content. The two model classes we study confabulate injected concepts that are high-frequency and concrete (e.g., "apple'"); for them correct concept guesses typically require significantly more tokens. This content-agnostic introspective mechanism is consistent with leading theories in philosophy and psychology.

</details>


### [166] [Distributed Partial Information Puzzles: Examining Common Ground Construction Under Epistemic Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05450)
*Yifan Zhu,Mariah Bradford,Kenneth Lai,Timothy Obiso,Videep Venkatesha,James Pustejovsky,Nikhil Krishnaswamy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了分布式部分信息拼图（DPIP）任务来研究多模态多智能体协作中的共同基础建立问题，创建了带标注的多模态数据集，并比较了LLM和基于动态认知逻辑的两种建模方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统在多模态、多方协作场景中建立共同基础（共享信念和相互认可的事实）仍然面临挑战，特别是在参与者拥有不同信息的认知不对称情况下。

Method: 1) 引入分布式部分信息拼图（DPIP）协作构建任务，激发认知不对称下的丰富多模态通信；2) 创建带标注的多模态数据集，在语音、手势和动作模态上进行时间对齐；3) 评估两种共同基础建模范式：基于提示的大语言模型和基于动态认知逻辑的公理化流程。

Result: DPIP任务对现代LLM在追踪任务进展和信念状态方面构成了挑战，表明当前模型在多模态多智能体协作中建立共同基础的能力有限。

Conclusion: 该研究为多模态协作中的共同基础建模提供了新的基准任务和数据集，揭示了当前LLM在此类复杂认知任务中的局限性，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Establishing common ground, a shared set of beliefs and mutually recognized facts, is fundamental to collaboration, yet remains a challenge for current AI systems, especially in multimodal, multiparty settings, where the collaborators bring different information to the table. We introduce the Distributed Partial Information Puzzle (DPIP), a collaborative construction task that elicits rich multimodal communication under epistemic asymmetry. We present a multimodal dataset of these interactions, annotated and temporally aligned across speech, gesture, and action modalities to support reasoning over propositional content and belief dynamics. We then evaluate two paradigms for modeling common ground (CG): (1) state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs), prompted to infer shared beliefs from multimodal updates, and (2) an axiomatic pipeline grounded in Dynamic Epistemic Logic (DEL) that incrementally performs the same task. Results on the annotated DPIP data indicate that it poses a challenge to modern LLMs' abilities to track both task progression and belief state.

</details>


### [167] [Towards Provably Unbiased LLM Judges via Bias-Bounded Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05485)
*Benjamin Feuer,Lucas Rosenblatt,Oussama Elachqar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出平均偏差有界性(A-BB)框架，为LLM评判系统提供形式化保证，减少可测量偏差带来的危害，在多种偏差设置下保持61-99%的原始排名相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统向自主反馈循环发展，需要可验证的奖励机制。在缺乏确定基准的场景中，LLM作为评判者成为实用方案，但现有系统无法提供强保证，特别是在未知或对抗性偏差存在时。

Method: 提出平均偏差有界性(A-BB)算法框架，为LLM评判系统提供形式化保证，确保任何可测量偏差都能减少危害/影响。在Arena-Hard-Auto数据集上评估，使用四个LLM评判者。

Result: 在格式化和模式偏差设置下，实现了(tau=0.5, delta=0.01)的偏差有界保证，同时保持61-99%的原始排名相关性，大多数评判者-偏差组合超过80%。

Conclusion: A-BB框架为LLM评判系统提供了形式化偏差控制保证，在保持评判质量的同时显著降低偏差影响，为自主AI系统的可靠反馈机制提供了解决方案。

Abstract: As AI models progress beyond simple chatbots into more complex workflows, we draw ever closer to the event horizon beyond which AI systems will be utilized in autonomous, self-maintaining feedback loops. Any autonomous AI system will depend on automated, verifiable rewards and feedback; in settings where ground truth is sparse or non-deterministic, one practical source of such rewards is an LLM-as-a-Judge. Although LLM judges continue to improve, the literature has yet to introduce systems capable of enforcing standards with strong guarantees, particularly when bias vectors are unknown or adversarially discovered. To remedy this issue, we propose average bias-boundedness (A-BB), an algorithmic framework which formally guarantees reductions of harm/impact as a result of any measurable bias in an LLM judge. Evaluating on Arena-Hard-Auto with four LLM judges, we achieve (tau=0.5, delta=0.01) bias-bounded guarantees while retaining 61-99% correlation with original rankings across formatting and schematic bias settings, with most judge-bias combinations exceeding 80%. The code to reproduce our findings is available at https://github.com/penfever/bias-bounded-evaluation.

</details>


### [168] [The Spike, the Sparse and the Sink: Anatomy of Massive Activations and Attention Sinks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05498)
*Shangwen Sun,Alfredo Canziani,Yann LeCun,Jiachen Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现Transformer语言模型中的大规模激活和注意力汇聚是两种相关但功能不同的现象，它们的共现主要是现代Transformer架构设计的产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Transformer语言模型中经常出现的两种现象：大规模激活（少数token在少数通道中表现出极端异常值）和注意力汇聚（某些token无论语义相关性如何都吸引不成比例的注意力）。先前工作观察到这两种现象经常共现且涉及相同token，但它们的功能作用和因果关系尚不清楚。

Method: 通过系统实验，分析这两种现象的功能角色和关系。特别研究了预归一化配置作为关键设计选择，并通过消融实验验证其对两种现象共现的影响。

Result: 大规模激活和注意力汇聚具有相关但不同的功能：大规模激活全局运作，诱导跨层持续存在的近乎恒定的隐藏表示，作为模型的隐式参数；注意力汇聚局部运作，调节注意力输出并偏向短程依赖。预归一化配置是导致两种现象共现的关键因素，消融该配置会使两种现象解耦。

Conclusion: Transformer语言模型中的大规模激活和注意力汇聚现象虽然经常共现，但具有不同的功能角色，它们的共现主要是现代Transformer架构设计（特别是预归一化配置）的产物，而非功能上的必然联系。

Abstract: We study two recurring phenomena in Transformer language models: massive activations, in which a small number of tokens exhibit extreme outliers in a few channels, and attention sinks, in which certain tokens attract disproportionate attention mass regardless of semantic relevance. Prior work observes that these phenomena frequently co-occur and often involve the same tokens, but their functional roles and causal relationship remain unclear. Through systematic experiments, we show that the co-occurrence is largely an architectural artifact of modern Transformer design, and that the two phenomena serve related but distinct functions. Massive activations operate globally: they induce near-constant hidden representations that persist across layers, effectively functioning as implicit parameters of the model. Attention sinks operate locally: they modulate attention outputs across heads and bias individual heads toward short-range dependencies. We identify the pre-norm configuration as the key choice that enables the co-occurrence, and show that ablating it causes the two phenomena to decouple.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [GAIDE: Graph-based Attention Masking for Spatial- and Embodiment-aware Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04463)
*Davood Soleymanzadeh,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GAIDE提出了一种基于图注意力掩码的神经采样器，通过编码运动规划问题的空间结构和机器人本体信息来指导采样，提高了规划效率和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于采样的运动规划算法在高维配置空间中存在采样效率低的问题，现有神经采样器难以有效编码运动规划问题中的空间结构信息。

Method: 将规划问题的空间结构和机器人本体信息表示为图结构，通过注意力掩码机制将其集成到基于Transformer的神经采样器中。

Result: 相比均匀采样、手工设计的启发式采样和现有神经采样方法，GAIDE在规划效率和成功率方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: GAIDE通过有效编码空间结构和本体信息，为高维机器人运动规划提供了更高效的神经采样解决方案。

Abstract: Sampling-based motion planning algorithms are widely used for motion planning of robotic manipulators, but they often struggle with sample inefficiency in high-dimensional configuration spaces due to their reliance on uniform or hand-crafted informed sampling primitives. Neural informed samplers address this limitation by learning the sampling distribution from prior planning experience to guide the motion planner towards planning goal. However, existing approaches often struggle to encode the spatial structure inherent in motion planning problems. To address this limitation, we introduce Graph-based Attention Masking for Spatial- and Embodiment-aware Motion Planning (GAIDE), a neural informed sampler that leverages both the spatial structure of the planning problem and the robotic manipulator's embodiment to guide the planning algorithm. GAIDE represents these structures as a graph and integrates it into a transformer-based neural sampler through attention masking. We evaluate GAIDE against baseline state-of-the-art sampling-based planners using uniform sampling, hand-crafted informed sampling, and neural informed sampling primitives. Evaluation results demonstrate that GAIDE improves planning efficiency and success rate.

</details>


### [170] [Act-Observe-Rewrite: Multimodal Coding Agents as In-Context Policy Learners for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04466)
*Vaishak Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Act-Observe-Rewrite (AOR)框架，让多模态语言模型通过观察失败并重写控制器代码来改进机器人操作策略，无需梯度更新、演示或奖励工程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多模态语言模型是否能够通过自我推理失败来学习操作物理对象，而不依赖传统的梯度更新、演示或奖励工程方法

Method: 提出AOR框架：LLM智能体在试验之间通过视觉观察和结构化结果指导，合成全新的可执行Python控制器代码，将底层运动控制实现作为LLM推理单元

Result: 在三个robosuite操作任务中验证，智能体无需演示、奖励工程或梯度更新就能达到高成功率

Conclusion: 可解释的代码作为策略表示创造了与不透明神经策略不同的上下文学习方式，智能体能够诊断系统性失败并重写其原因

Abstract: Can a multimodal language model learn to manipulate physical objects by reasoning about its own failures-without gradient updates, demonstrations, or reward engineering? We argue the answer is yes, under conditions we characterise precisely. We present Act-Observe-Rewrite (AOR), a framework in which an LLM agent improves a robot manipulation policy by synthesising entirely new executable Python controller code between trials, guided by visual observations and structured episode outcomes. Unlike prior work that grounds LLMs in pre-defined skill libraries or uses code generation for one-shot plan synthesis, AOR makes the full low-level motor control implementation the unit of LLM reasoning, enabling the agent to change not just what the robot does, but how it does it. The central claim is that interpretable code as the policy representation creates a qualitatively different kind of in-context learning from opaque neural policies: the agent can diagnose systematic failures and rewrite their causes. We validate this across three robosuite manipulation tasks and report promising results, with the agent achieving high success rates without demonstrations, reward engineering, or gradient updates.

</details>


### [171] [Efficient Autonomous Navigation of a Quadruped Robot in Underground Mines on Edge Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04470)
*Yixiang Gao,Kwame Awuah-Offei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种完全自主的导航系统，用于波士顿动力Spot四足机器人在地下矿井中运行，无需GPU或网络连接，在低功耗边缘计算机上实现实时感知到动作控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下矿井导航面临狭窄通道、不平坦地形、黑暗、GPS受限和通信基础设施有限等挑战，现有基于学习的方法需要GPU加速和大量训练数据，需要开发能在低功耗设备上运行的自主导航系统。

Method: 集成激光雷达惯性里程计、扫描匹配定位、地形分割、可见性图全局规划和速度调节局部路径跟随器，在单次环境建图后，系统无需环境特定训练即可处理任意目标位置。

Result: 在实验性地下矿井中进行了20次试验（4个目标位置×5次重复），累计超过700米完全自主遍历，成功率达100%，整体SPL为0.73±0.09。

Conclusion: 开发了一种完全自主的导航系统，可在低功耗边缘计算机上运行，无需GPU或网络连接，成功验证了在地下矿井环境中的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Abstract: Embodied navigation in underground mines faces significant challenges, including narrow passages, uneven terrain, near-total darkness, GPS-denied conditions, and limited communication infrastructure. While recent learning-based approaches rely on GPU-accelerated inference and extensive training data, we present a fully autonomous navigation stack for a Boston Dynamics Spot quadruped robot that runs entirely on a low-power Intel NUC edge computer with no GPU and no network connectivity requirements. The system integrates LiDAR-inertial odometry, scan-matching localization against a prior map, terrain segmentation, and visibility-graph global planning with a velocity-regulated local path follower, achieving real-time perception-to-action at consistent control rates. After a single mapping pass of the environment, the system handles arbitrary goal locations within the known map without any environment-specific training or learned components. We validate the system through repeated field trials using four target locations of varying traversal difficulty in an experimental underground mine, accumulating over 700 m of fully autonomous traverse with a 100% success rate across all 20 trials (5 repetitions x 4 targets) and an overall Success weighted by Path Length (SPL) of 0.73 \pm 0.09.

</details>


### [172] [PTLD: Sim-to-real Privileged Tactile Latent Distillation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04531)
*Rosy Chen,Mustafa Mukadam,Michael Kaess,Tingfan Wu,Francois R Hogan,Jitendra Malik,Akash Sharma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PTLD是一种无需触觉模拟的sim-to-real方法，通过在真实世界中使用特权传感器收集触觉数据，蒸馏出基于触觉输入的鲁棒状态估计器，显著提升模拟训练的策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉灵巧操作对自动化复杂家务任务至关重要，但学习有效控制策略面临挑战：模仿学习需要高质量演示数据难以获取，而强化学习在模拟中训练时，快速逼真的触觉观测模拟又很困难。

Method: 提出PTLD方法：不模拟触觉传感器，也不依赖纯本体感知策略进行零样本sim-to-real迁移，而是在真实世界中使用特权传感器收集真实触觉策略数据，然后用这些数据蒸馏出基于触觉输入的鲁棒状态估计器。

Result: 在基准手内旋转任务中，PTLD比纯本体感知策略性能提升182%；在更具挑战性的触觉手内重定向任务中，达到目标的数量比纯本体感知方法提升57%。

Conclusion: PTLD能够显著提升模拟训练的本体感知操作策略性能，通过融入触觉感知实现更好的sim-to-real迁移，无需触觉模拟即可学习触觉操作技能。

Abstract: Tactile dexterous manipulation is essential to automating complex household tasks, yet learning effective control policies remains a challenge. While recent work has relied on imitation learning, obtaining high quality demonstrations for multi-fingered hands via robot teleoperation or kinesthetic teaching is prohibitive. Alternatively, with reinforcement we can learn skills in simulation, but fast and realistic simulation of tactile observations is challenging. To bridge this gap, we introduce PTLD: sim-to-real Privileged Tactile Latent Distillation, a novel approach to learning tactile manipulation skills without requiring tactile simulation. Instead of simulating tactile sensors or relying purely on proprioceptive policies to transfer zero-shot sim-to-real, our key idea is to leverage privileged sensors in the real world to collect real-world tactile policy data. This data is then used to distill a robust state estimator that operates on tactile input. We demonstrate from our experiments that PTLD can be used to improve proprioceptive manipulation policies trained in simulation significantly by incorporating tactile sensing. On the benchmark in-hand rotation task, PTLD achieves a 182% improvement over a proprioception only policy. We also show that PTLD enables learning the challenging task of tactile in-hand reorientation where we see a 57% improvement in the number of goals reached over using proprioception alone. Website: https://akashsharma02.github.io/ptld-website/.

</details>


### [173] [Many-RRT*: Robust Joint-Space Trajectory Planning for Serial Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04547)
*Theodore M. Belmont,Benjamin A. Christie,Anton Netchaev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Many-RRT*：一种并行规划到多个目标的RRT*扩展方法，通过同时从多个逆运动学解生长独立树，解决高自由度机械臂关节空间规划中的多解问题，显著提高轨迹质量和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高自由度串联机械臂的快速发展需要快速采样式运动规划器。然而，在关节空间规划面临挑战：单一任务空间末端执行器姿态对应多个配置空间状态（逆运动学多解），形成多臂老虎机问题。选择错误的关节空间目标会导致次优轨迹甚至规划失败。

Method: 提出Many-RRT*：RRT*-Connect的扩展，并行规划到多个目标。通过生成多个逆运动学解，从这些目标配置同时生长独立树（与单个起始树并行），确保计算资源不浪费在次优逆运动学解上。该方法保持鲁棒收敛性和渐近最优性。

Result: 在不同机器人形态和多样化障碍环境的实验评估表明：Many-RRT*在相同运行时间内提供更高质量轨迹（成本降低44.5%），成功率显著更高（100% vs 次优方法的1.6%），且不影响运行时间性能。

Conclusion: Many-RRT*通过并行处理多个逆运动学解，有效解决了高自由度机械臂关节空间规划中的多解问题，在轨迹质量、成功率和计算效率方面均优于传统RRT变体，为复杂环境中的机械臂运动规划提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of high degree-of-freedom (DoF) serial manipulators necessitates the use of swift, sampling-based motion planners for high-dimensional spaces. While sampling-based planners like the Rapidly-Exploring Random Tree (RRT) are widely used, planning in the manipulator's joint space presents significant challenges due to non-invertible forward kinematics. A single task-space end-effector pose can correspond to multiple configuration-space states, creating a multi-arm bandit problem for the planner. In complex environments, simply choosing the wrong joint space goal can result in suboptimal trajectories or even failure to find a viable plan. To address this planning problem, we propose Many-RRT*: an extension of RRT*-Connect that plans to multiple goals in parallel. By generating multiple IK solutions and growing independent trees from these goal configurations simultaneously alongside a single start tree, Many-RRT* ensures that computational effort is not wasted on suboptimal IK solutions. This approach maintains robust convergence and asymptotic optimality. Experimental evaluations across robot morphologies and diverse obstacle environments demonstrate that Many-RRT* provides higher quality trajectories (44.5% lower cost in the same runtime) with a significantly higher success rate (100% vs. the next best of 1.6%) than previous RRT iterations without compromising on runtime performance.

</details>


### [174] [From Local Corrections to Generalized Skills: Improving Neuro-Symbolic Policies with MEMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04560)
*Benjamin A. Christie,Yinlong Dai,Mohammad Bararjanianbahnamiri,Simon Stepputtis,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MEMO：通过收集、聚类和重述多人多任务的自然语言纠错反馈，构建检索增强的技能库，使机器人能够动态扩展技能并泛化到新任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号框架依赖预定义技能库，当机器人无法生成适合当前任务的技能时，策略会失败。需要一种方法让机器人能够从人类反馈中动态学习和扩展技能

Method: MEMO系统收集多人多任务的自然语言纠错反馈，通过聚类和重述合成更通用的文本指导和代码技能模板，构建检索增强的技能库。运行时检索相关文本和代码，使策略能够生成新技能

Result: MEMO能够将局部反馈聚合为通用技能模板，在现有基线方法失败的场景中，成功泛化到新任务

Conclusion: 通过收集和整合多人多任务的人类反馈，MEMO能够动态扩展机器人技能库，解决神经符号框架中技能约束的瓶颈问题，实现更好的任务泛化能力

Abstract: Recent works use a neuro-symbolic framework for general manipulation policies. The advantage of this framework is that -- by applying off-the-shelf vision and language models -- the robot can break complex tasks down into semantic subtasks. However, the fundamental bottleneck is that the robot needs skills to ground these subtasks into embodied motions. Skills can take many forms (e.g., trajectory snippets, motion primitives, coded functions), but regardless of their form skills act as a constraint. The high-level policy can only ground its language reasoning through the available skills; if the robot cannot generate the right skill for the current task, its policy will fail. We propose to address this limitation -- and dynamically expand the robot's skills -- by leveraging user feedback. When a robot fails, humans can intuitively explain what went wrong (e.g., ``no, go higher''). While a simple approach is to recall this exact text the next time the robot faces a similar situation, we hypothesize that by collecting, clustering, and re-phrasing natural language corrections across multiple users and tasks, we can synthesize more general text guidance and coded skill templates. Applying this hypothesis we develop Memory Enhanced Manipulation (MEMO). MEMO builds and maintains a retrieval-augmented skillbook gathered from human feedback and task successes. At run time, MEMO retrieves relevant text and code from this skillbook, enabling the robot's policy to generate new skills while reasoning over multi-task human feedback. Our experiments demonstrate that using MEMO to aggregate local feedback into general skill templates enables generalization to novel tasks where existing baselines fall short. See supplemental material here: https://collab.me.vt.edu/memo

</details>


### [175] [Distributed State Estimation for Vision-Based Cooperative Slung Load Transportation in GPS-Denied Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04571)
*Jack R. Pence,Jackson Fezell,Jack W. Langelaan,Junyi Geng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于视觉的分布式去中心化多旋翼协同吊运系统，使用单目相机和分布式扩展信息滤波器实现GPS拒止环境下的鲁棒载荷状态估计与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单机吊运系统存在载荷容量和控制权限限制，而现有协同吊运研究大多依赖GPS或集中式架构，缺乏在GPS拒止或受限环境中的鲁棒性。

Method: 采用分布式去中心化扩展信息滤波器(DDEIF)，各无人机通过单目相机检测载荷上的基准标记并估计相对位姿，然后融合测量值实现鲁棒状态估计，用于闭环轨迹跟踪控制。

Result: Gazebo中的蒙特卡洛仿真结果表明该方法有效，包括在飞行中通信丢失情况下的性能表现。

Conclusion: 提出的分布式去中心化框架为GPS拒止环境下的视觉协同吊运提供了鲁棒且可扩展的解决方案，增强了系统对传感器丢失的容错能力。

Abstract: Transporting heavy or oversized slung loads using rotorcraft has traditionally relied on single-aircraft systems, which limits both payload capacity and control authority. Cooperative multilift using teams of rotorcraft offers a scalable and efficient alternative, especially for infrequent but challenging "long-tail" payloads without the need of building larger and larger rotorcraft. Most prior multilift research assumes GPS availability, uses centralized estimation architectures, or relies on controlled laboratory motion-capture setups. As a result, these methods lack robustness to sensor loss and are not viable in GPS-denied or operationally constrained environments. This paper addresses this limitation by presenting a distributed and decentralized payload state estimation framework for vision-based multilift operations. Using onboard monocular cameras, each UAV detects a fiducial marker on the payload and estimates its relative pose. These measurements are fused via a Distributed and Decentralized Extended Information Filter (DDEIF), enabling robust and scalable estimation that is resilient to individual sensor dropouts. This payload state estimate is then used for closed-loop trajectory tracking control. Monte Carlo simulation results in Gazebo show the effectiveness of the proposed approach, including the effect of communication loss during flight.

</details>


### [176] [Risk-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04579)
*Michael Groom,James Wilson,Nick Hawes,Lars Kunze*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个基于模仿学习的风险感知视觉运动策略，用于移动机械臂在未知环境中进行全身控制，通过分布强化学习训练教师策略，再蒸馏到仅依赖深度观测的学生策略，实现可调节风险敏感度的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动机械臂全身控制器缺乏在不确定性下的风险敏感决策机制，而机器人从实验室过渡到日常环境需要能够评估行动风险并做出风险感知决策，特别是在动态非结构化空间中进行移动操作任务时。

Method: 1) 使用分布强化学习训练特权教师策略，采用风险中性分布评论家；2) 应用扭曲风险度量到评论家预测的回报分布，计算风险调整优势估计用于策略更新；3) 通过模仿学习将教师策略蒸馏到仅依赖自中心深度观测的学生策略。

Result: 训练出的视觉运动策略在未映射环境中表现出风险感知行为，特别是在最坏情况性能方面表现更好，能够利用实时深度观测进行感知，执行反应式全身运动。

Conclusion: 首次实现了基于自中心深度观测的风险感知视觉运动策略学习，通过模仿学习成功转移风险感知行为，为移动机械臂在动态非结构化环境中的安全操作提供了新方法。

Abstract: For robots to successfully transition from lab settings to everyday environments, they must begin to reason about the risks associated with their actions and make informed, risk-aware decisions. This is particularly true for robots performing mobile manipulation tasks, which involve both interacting with and navigating within dynamic, unstructured spaces. However, existing whole-body controllers for mobile manipulators typically lack explicit mechanisms for risk-sensitive decision-making under uncertainty. To our knowledge, we are the first to (i) learn risk-aware visuomotor policies for mobile manipulation conditioned on egocentric depth observations with runtime-adjustable risk sensitivity, and (ii) show risk-aware behaviours can be transferred through Imitation Learning (IL) to a visuomotor policy conditioned on egocentric depth observations. Our method achieves this by first training a privileged teacher policy using Distributional Reinforcement Learning (DRL), with a risk-neutral distributional critic. Distortion risk-metrics are then applied to the critic's predicted return distribution to calculate risk-adjusted advantage estimates used in policy updates to achieve a range of risk-aware behaviours. We then distil teacher policies with IL to obtain risk-aware student policies conditioned on egocentric depth observations. We perform extensive evaluations demonstrating that our trained visuomotor policies exhibit risk-aware behaviour (specifically achieving better worst-case performance) while performing reactive whole-body motions in unmapped environments, leveraging live depth observations for perception.

</details>


### [177] [ELLIPSE: Evidential Learning for Robust Waypoints and Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04585)
*Zihao Dong,Chanyoung Chung,Dong-Ki Kim,Mukhtar Maulimov,Xiangyun Meng,Harmish Khambhaita,Ali-akbar Agha-mohammadi,Amirreza Shaban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ELLIPSE：基于深度证据回归的鲁棒航点预测方法，通过多元Student-t分布输出航点和不确定性，使用领域增强和校准技术提高分布偏移下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在开放世界安全关键环境中需要鲁棒的航点预测。模仿学习方法虽然成功但容易受到分布偏移影响，在陌生状态下可能变得过度自信，导致危险行为。

Method: 1. 基于多元深度证据回归输出航点和多元Student-t预测分布；2. 引入轻量级领域增强程序，合成合理的视点/姿态变化而不需要额外演示数据；3. 应用后验等渗校准概率积分变换值，提高环境/领域偏移下的不确定性可靠性。

Result: 在真实世界评估中，ELLIPSE相比基线方法提高了任务成功率和不确定性覆盖率，特别是在楼梯航点预测任务中表现出色。

Conclusion: ELLIPSE通过结合深度证据回归、领域增强和校准技术，为移动机器人提供了在分布偏移下更鲁棒的航点预测和不确定性估计方法。

Abstract: Robust waypoint prediction is crucial for mobile robots operating in open-world, safety-critical settings. While Imitation Learning (IL) methods have demonstrated great success in practice, they are susceptible to distribution shifts: the policy can become dangerously overconfident in unfamiliar states. In this paper, we present \textit{ELLIPSE}, a method building on multivariate deep evidential regression to output waypoints and multivariate Student-t predictive distributions in a single forward pass. To reduce covariate-shift-induced overconfidence under viewpoint and pose perturbations near expert trajectories, we introduce a lightweight domain augmentation procedure that synthesizes plausible viewpoint/pose variations without collecting additional demonstrations. To improve uncertainty reliability under environment/domain shift (e.g., unseen staircases), we apply a post-hoc isotonic recalibration on probability integral transform (PIT) values so that prediction sets remain plausible during deployment. We ground the discussion and experiments in staircase waypoint prediction, where obtaining robust waypoint and uncertainty is pivotal. Extensive real world evaluations show that \textit{ELLIPSE} improves both task success rate and uncertainty coverage compared to baselines.

</details>


### [178] [RoboMME: Benchmarking and Understanding Memory for Robotic Generalist Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04639)
*Yinpei Dai,Hongze Fu,Jayjun Lee,Yuejiang Liu,Haoran Zhang,Jianing Yang,Chelsea Finn,Nima Fazeli,Joyce Chai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了RoboMME基准测试，用于评估和推进VLA模型在长时程、历史依赖场景中的表现，包含16个任务和14个记忆增强VLA变体


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆对于长时程和历史依赖的机器人操作至关重要，但现有VLA模型的记忆机制评估局限于狭窄、非标准化的设置，限制了系统理解、比较和进展测量

Method: 引入RoboMME大规模标准化基准，包含16个基于精心设计分类法的操作任务，评估时间、空间、对象和程序记忆；开发14个基于π0.5骨干的记忆增强VLA变体，探索不同记忆表示和集成策略

Result: 实验结果显示记忆表示的有效性高度任务依赖，每种设计在不同任务中都有独特的优势和局限性

Conclusion: RoboMME基准为系统评估和推进VLA模型在长时程、历史依赖场景中的记忆能力提供了标准化框架，揭示了记忆表示设计的任务依赖性

Abstract: Memory is critical for long-horizon and history-dependent robotic manipulation. Such tasks often involve counting repeated actions or manipulating objects that become temporarily occluded. Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models have begun to incorporate memory mechanisms; however, their evaluations remain confined to narrow, non-standardized settings. This limits their systematic understanding, comparison, and progress measurement. To address these challenges, we introduce RoboMME: a large-scale standardized benchmark for evaluating and advancing VLA models in long-horizon, history-dependent scenarios. Our benchmark comprises 16 manipulation tasks constructed under a carefully designed taxonomy that evaluates temporal, spatial, object, and procedural memory. We further develop a suite of 14 memory-augmented VLA variants built on the π0.5 backbone to systematically explore different memory representations across multiple integration strategies. Experimental results show that the effectiveness of memory representations is highly task-dependent, with each design offering distinct advantages and limitations across different tasks. Videos and code can be found at our website https://robomme.github.io.

</details>


### [179] [Autonomous Aerial Non-Destructive Testing: Ultrasound Inspection with a Commercial Quadrotor in an Unstructured Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04642)
*Ruben Veenstra,Barbara Bazzana,Sander Smits,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了首个基于商用多旋翼的完全自主接触式无损检测系统，通过实时框架和多速率控制方案实现自主物理交互


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统商用多旋翼仅支持遥控操作，无法实现自主接触式无损检测。需要开发能够将现成平台改造成支持自主物理交互的系统，以实现危险和受限基础设施的安全接触式检测。

Method: 开发了实时框架直接与机载传感器套件接口，采用多速率控制方案：200Hz的低级控制、100Hz的力估计、50Hz的导纳滤波器和轨迹规划器，最终向内部飞行控制器提供加速度和偏航率指令。

Result: 在配备超声波载荷的Flyability Elios 3四旋翼上进行物理实验验证，仅依靠机载传感，无人机成功在非结构化工业环境中执行自主无损检测测量。

Conclusion: 这项工作证明了将现成平台改造用于自主物理交互的可行性，为危险和受限基础设施的安全接触式检测铺平了道路。

Abstract: This work presents an integrated control and software architecture that enables arguably the first fully autonomous, contact-based non-destructive testing (NDT) using a commercial multirotor originally restricted to remotely-piloted operations. To allow autonomous operation with an off-the-shelf platform, we developed a real-time framework that interfaces directly with its onboard sensor suite. The architecture features a multi-rate control scheme: low-level control is executed at 200 Hz, force estimation at 100 Hz, while an admittance filter and trajectory planner operate at 50 Hz, ultimately supplying acceleration and yaw rate commands to the internal flight controller. We validate the system through physical experiments on a Flyability Elios 3 quadrotor equipped with an ultrasound payload. Relying exclusively on onboard sensing, the vehicle successfully performs autonomous NDT measurements within an unstructured, industrial-like environment. This work demonstrates the viability of retrofitting off-the-shelf platforms for autonomous physical interaction, paving the way for safe, contact-based inspection of hazardous and confined infrastructure.

</details>


### [180] [GIANT - Global Path Integration and Attentive Graph Networks for Multi-Agent Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04659)
*Jonas le Fevre Sejersen,Toyotaro Suzumura,Erdal Kayacan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合全局路径规划与局部导航的多机器人避碰方法，使用注意力图神经网络处理动态交互，通过训练中引入噪声增强鲁棒性，在复杂动态环境中表现优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人在复杂动态环境中的导航面临挑战，现有方法在适应环境变化和避免碰撞方面存在不足，需要一种既能遵循全局最优路径又能动态调整的鲁棒解决方案。

Method: 提出集成全局路径规划与局部导航的方法，利用注意力图神经网络管理机器人间的动态交互，局部导航模型基于预规划的全局路径，训练时引入噪声增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在多种结构多样的仿真场景中评估，相比NH-ORCA、DRL-NAV、GA3C-CADRL等基线方法，本方法获得更高的成功率、更低的碰撞率和更高效的导航性能，尤其在挑战性场景中优势明显。

Conclusion: 该方法在多机器人导航领域取得进展，为物流等复杂动态环境中的鲁棒性能提供了解决方案，能够适应意外障碍和不可预测的变化。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach to multi-robot collision avoidance that integrates global path planning with local navigation strategies, utilizing attentive graph neural networks to manage dynamic interactions among agents. We introduce a local navigation model that leverages pre-planned global paths, allowing robots to adhere to optimal routes while dynamically adjusting to environmental changes. The models robustness is enhanced through the introduction of noise during training, resulting in superior performance in complex, dynamic environments. Our approach is evaluated against established baselines, including NH-ORCA, DRL-NAV, and GA3C-CADRL, across various structurally diverse simulated scenarios. The results demonstrate that our model achieves consistently higher success rates, lower collision rates, and more efficient navigation, particularly in challenging scenarios where baseline models struggle. This work offers an advancement in multi-robot navigation, with implications for robust performance in complex, dynamic environments with varying degrees of complexity, such as those encountered in logistics, where adaptability is essential for accommodating unforeseen obstacles and unpredictable changes.

</details>


### [181] [Python Bindings for a Large C++ Robotics Library: The Case of OMPL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04668)
*Weihang Guo,Theodoros Tyrovouzis,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究使用大型语言模型辅助生成C++库的Python绑定，通过专家审查确保正确性，并在运动规划库案例中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python绑定是连接高性能C++库和Python灵活性的关键桥梁，但为大型代码库生成绑定是一个繁琐的过程，给维护者带来沉重负担。

Method: 采用LLMs辅助生成nanobind包装器，保持专家在循环中。工作流程包括：镜像C++代码库结构、搭建空包装文件、使用LLMs填充绑定定义，然后专家审查和优化生成的代码。

Result: 通过大型C++运动规划库的案例研究，记录了常见失败模式（如共享指针管理、重载、跳板函数等），并展示了上下文示例和精心设计的提示如何提高可靠性。生成的绑定在运行时性能上与遗留解决方案相当。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅适用于特定案例，还为在大型C++项目中应用LLMs进行绑定生成提供了通用经验教训，证明了LLMs辅助生成Python绑定的可行性。

Abstract: Python bindings are a critical bridge between high-performance C++ libraries and the flexibility of Python, enabling rapid prototyping, reproducible experiments, and integration with simulation and learning frameworks in robotics research. Yet, generating bindings for large codebases is a tedious process that creates a heavy burden for a small group of maintainers. In this work, we investigate the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) to assist in generating nanobind wrappers, with human experts kept in the loop. Our workflow mirrors the structure of the C++ codebase, scaffolds empty wrapper files, and employs LLMs to fill in binding definitions. Experts then review and refine the generated code to ensure correctness, compatibility, and performance. Through a case study on a large C++ motion planning library, we document common failure modes, including mismanaging shared pointers, overloads, and trampolines, and show how in-context examples and careful prompt design improve reliability. Experiments demonstrate that the resulting bindings achieve runtime performance comparable to legacy solutions. Beyond this case study, our results provide general lessons for applying LLMs to binding generation in large-scale C++ projects.

</details>


### [182] [Selecting Spots by Explicitly Predicting Intention from Motion History Improves Performance in Autonomous Parking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04695)
*Long Kiu Chung,David Isele,Faizan M. Tariq,Sangjae Bae,Shreyas Kousik,Jovin D'sa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自动驾驶代客泊车系统，通过显式预测其他车辆意图来选择泊车位，优于仅依赖运动预测或端到端模型的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶代客泊车场景中，由于停车场驾驶规则相对宽松，仅通过短期运动预测难以可靠推断多样且模糊的长期目标，需要显式意图预测来做出更安全、社会接受度更高的决策。

Method: 构建AVP流程，通过学习模型和概率信念图从车辆运动历史中显式预测其他车辆的泊车意图，并在包含反应式代理和遮挡感知观察、不完美轨迹预测等现实假设的仿真环境中测试。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该方法在预测准确性、社会接受度和任务完成率方面优于仅从未来预测运动推断意图或将其隐式嵌入端到端模型的现有方法。

Conclusion: 在泊车场景中，显式意图预测对于推理多样且模糊的长期目标至关重要，这些目标无法仅从短期运动预测可靠推断，但可以从运动历史中有效学习。

Abstract: In many applications of social navigation, existing works have shown that predicting and reasoning about human intentions can help robotic agents make safer and more socially acceptable decisions. In this work, we study this problem for autonomous valet parking (AVP), where an autonomous vehicle ego agent must drop off its passengers, explore the parking lot, find a parking spot, negotiate for the spot with other vehicles, and park in the spot without human supervision. Specifically, we propose an AVP pipeline that selects parking spots by explicitly predicting where other agents are going to park from their motion history using learned models and probabilistic belief maps. To test this pipeline, we build a simulation environment with reactive agents and realistic modeling assumptions on the ego agent, such as occlusion-aware observations, and imperfect trajectory prediction. Simulation experiments show that our proposed method outperforms existing works that infer intentions from future predicted motion or embed them implicitly in end-to-end models, yielding better results in prediction accuracy, social acceptance, and task completion. Our key insight is that, in parking, where driving regulations are more lax, explicit intention prediction is crucial for reasoning about diverse and ambiguous long-term goals, which cannot be reliably inferred from short-term motion prediction alone, but can be effectively learned from motion history.

</details>


### [183] [LEGS-POMDP: Language and Gesture-Guided Object Search in Partially Observable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04705)
*Ivy Xiao He,Stefanie Tellex,Jason Xinyu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LEGS-POMDP系统，整合语言、手势和视觉观测，用于开放世界物体搜索，解决目标物体身份和空间位置的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在开放世界环境中需要解释模糊指令来定位目标物体。现有方法中，基础模型擅长多模态接地但缺乏不确定性建模机制，而POMDPs能系统规划但模态有限且依赖严格环境假设。

Method: 提出LEGS-POMDP模块化系统，整合语言、手势和视觉观测，显式建模目标物体身份和空间位置两种部分可观测性来源。

Result: 仿真中多模态融合显著优于单模态基线，在挑战性环境和物体类别上平均成功率89%。四足移动机械臂真实实验验证了鲁棒的多模态感知和模糊指令下的不确定性降低。

Conclusion: LEGS-POMDP成功整合了基础模型的多模态能力和POMDPs的不确定性规划框架，为开放世界物体搜索提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: To assist humans in open-world environments, robots must interpret ambiguous instructions to locate desired objects. Foundation model-based approaches excel at multimodal grounding, but they lack a principled mechanism for modeling uncertainty in long-horizon tasks. In contrast, Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) provide a systematic framework for planning under uncertainty but are often limited in supported modalities and rely on restrictive environment assumptions. We introduce LanguagE and Gesture-Guided Object Search in Partially Observable Environments (LEGS-POMDP), a modular POMDP system that integrates language, gesture, and visual observations for open-world object search. Unlike prior work, LEGS-POMDP explicitly models two sources of partial observability: uncertainty over the target object's identity and its spatial location. In simulation, multimodal fusion significantly outperforms unimodal baselines, achieving an average success rate of 89\% across challenging environments and object categories. Finally, we demonstrate the full system on a quadruped mobile manipulator, where real-world experiments qualitatively validate robust multimodal perception and uncertainty reduction under ambiguous instructions.

</details>


### [184] [Design, Mapping, and Contact Anticipation with 3D-printed Whole-Body Tactile and Proximity Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04714)
*Carson Kohlbrenner,Anna Soukhovei,Caleb Escobedo,Nataliya Nechyporenko,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GenTact-Prox是一种集成触觉和接近感应的3D打印人工皮肤，可覆盖机器人全身，实现接触检测和预测，感知范围达18厘米


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在动态共享环境中运行时，需要在接触发生前进行预测，以提高安全性和操作效率

Method: 开发模块化、程序生成的3D打印人工皮肤平台，集成触觉和接近感应；引入数据驱动框架映射感知空间，在Franka Research 3机器人上部署5个单元进行验证

Result: 人工皮肤检测范围达18厘米，成功实现在线物体感知操作和接触预测，能够覆盖机器人全身

Conclusion: GenTact-Prox为机器人提供了接触前感知能力，通过感知空间映射实现了更智能、更安全的操作

Abstract: Robots operating in dynamic and shared environments benefit from anticipating contact before it occurs. We present GenTact-Prox, a fully 3D-printed artificial skin that integrates tactile and proximity sensing for contact detection and anticipation. The artificial skin platform is modular in design, procedurally generated to fit any robot morphology, and can cover the whole body of a robot. The skin achieved detection ranges of up to 18 cm during evaluation. To characterize how robots perceive nearby space through this skin, we introduce a data-driven framework for mapping the Perisensory Space -- the body-centric volume of space around the robot where sensors provide actionable information for contact anticipation. We demonstrate this approach on a Franka Research 3 robot equipped with five GenTact-Prox units, enabling online object-aware operation and contact prediction.

</details>


### [185] [Gait Generation Balancing Joint Load and Mobility for Legged Modular Robots with Easily Detachable Joints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04757)
*Kennosuke Chihara,Takuya Kiyokawa,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于NSGA-III算法的优化框架，在保证移动速度和稳定性的同时最小化关节负载，解决模块化机器人运动中关节扭矩过大导致的机械失效风险


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化机器人在运动过程中关节扭矩过大会导致机械失效风险，特别是对于可拆卸关节。传统方法只关注移动性，忽视了关节负载问题。

Method: 使用NSGA-III多目标优化算法，在移动速度、稳定性和关节负载之间寻找帕累托最优解，生成适合不同环境（如斜坡、台阶）的步态运动。

Result: 仿真和物理实验表明，该方法能成功生成适应多种环境的步态运动，在保持整体移动性的同时确保结构完整性。

Conclusion: 提出的优化框架有效解决了模块化机器人关节负载问题，在移动性能和结构完整性之间取得了良好平衡，提高了机器人的可靠性和耐用性。

Abstract: While modular robots offer versatility, excessive joint torque during locomotion poses a significant risk of mechanical failure, especially for detachable joints. To address this, we propose an optimization framework using the NSGA-III algorithm. Unlike conventional approaches that prioritize mobility alone, our method derives Pareto optimal solutions to minimize joint load while maintaining necessary locomotion speed and stability. Simulations and physical experiments demonstrate that our approach successfully generates gait motions for diverse environments, such as slopes and steps, ensuring structural integrity without compromising overall mobility.

</details>


### [186] [Designing and Validating a Self-Aligning Tool Changer for Modular Reconfigurable Manipulation Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04760)
*Mahfudz Maskur,Takuya Kiyokawa,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种容错工具更换系统，通过被动自对准几何结构补偿定位误差，实现模块化机器人可靠自主工具更换


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统刚性主动耦合机构因不可避免的定位和方向误差而经常失效，模块化可重构机器人需要可靠的自动模块交换机制

Method: 采用电机驱动耦合机构，利用斜面插座和三角形引导结构等被动自对准几何特征，无需复杂力传感器即可补偿角度和横向偏差；配合紧凑旋转工具交换站实现高效模块存储

Result: 真实世界自主工具拾取实验验证了自对准特征能成功吸收执行误差，实现高度可靠的机器人工具重构

Conclusion: 提出的容错工具更换系统通过被动自对准机制有效解决了模块化机器人自动模块交换中的定位误差问题

Abstract: Modular reconfigurable robots require reliable mechanisms for automated module exchange, but conventional rigid active couplings often fail due to inevitable positioning and orientational errors. To address this, we propose a misalignment-tolerant tool-changing system. The hardware features a motor-driven coupling utilizing passive self-alignment geometries, specifically chamfered receptacles and triangular lead-in guides, to robustly compensate for angular and lateral misalignments without complex force sensors. To make this autonomous exchange practically feasible, the mechanism is complemented by a compact rotating tool exchange station for efficient module storage. Real-world autonomous tool-picking experiments validate that the self-aligning features successfully absorb execution errors, enabling highly reliable robotic tool reconfiguration.

</details>


### [187] [Adaptive Policy Switching of Two-Wheeled Differential Robots for Traversing over Diverse Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04761)
*Haruki Izawa,Takeshi Takai,Shingo Kitano,Mikita Miyaguchi,Hiroaki Kawashima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究通过分析机器人在模拟月球熔岩管环境中导航时的姿态数据，发现俯仰角的标准差能有效区分平坦和崎岖地形，使用70步窗口的GMM分类准确率超过98%，为自适应策略切换提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球熔岩管探索需要机器人自主导航，但预训练策略无法覆盖所有地形条件，因此需要基于当前地形特征自适应切换专门化策略，这需要有效的地形类型估计方法。

Method: 使用PPO微调预训练策略，在模拟熔岩管环境中收集机器人在平坦和崎岖地形上的3D姿态数据，分析俯仰角标准差的地形区分能力，采用高斯混合模型在不同窗口大小下进行地形分类评估。

Result: 机器人俯仰角标准差在平坦和崎岖地形间有明显差异，使用70步窗口的GMM分类准确率达到98%以上，表明短期姿态数据足以可靠估计地形类型。

Conclusion: 基于姿态观测的地形分类方法有效可行，为机器人自适应策略切换提供了可靠基础，使机器人能根据当前地形特征选择合适的专门化控制策略。

Abstract: Exploring lunar lava tubes requires robots to traverse without human intervention. Because pre-trained policies cannot fully cover all possible terrain conditions, our goal is to enable adaptive policy switching, where the robot selects an appropriate terrain-specialized model based on its current terrain features. This study investigates whether terrain types can be estimated effectively using posture-related observations collected during navigation. We fine-tuned a pre-trained policy using Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), and then collected the robot's 3D orientation data as it moved across flat and rough terrain in a simulated lava-tube environment. Our analysis revealed that the standard deviation of the robot's pitch data shows a clear difference between these two terrain types. Using Gaussian mixture models (GMM), we evaluated terrain classification across various window sizes. An accuracy of more than 98% was achieved when using a 70-step window. The result suggests that short-term orientation data are sufficient for reliable terrain estimation, providing a foundation for adaptive policy switching.

</details>


### [188] [LLM-Guided Decentralized Exploration with Self-Organizing Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04762)
*Hiroaki Kawashima,Shun Ikejima,Takeshi Takai,Mikita Miyaguchi,Yasuharu Kunii*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结合自组织团队形成和LLM目标决策的多机器人探索方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个机器人感知能力有限且容错性不足时，需要多机器人组队以扩大观测范围和提升可靠性。传统集中控制方法缺乏鲁棒性和灵活性，需要自主的团队形成和目标决策机制。

Method: 1) 自组织算法实现自主动态的多团队形成；2) 基于大语言模型(LLM)的算法让每个团队自主确定下一个探索目标。与传统的基于前沿的方法和深度强化学习方法形成对比。

Result: 通过数十到数百个机器人的仿真验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 结合自组织团队形成和LLM目标决策的方法能够实现高效的多机器人探索，在缺乏集中控制的情况下仍能保持鲁棒性和灵活性。

Abstract: When individual robots have limited sensing capabilities or insufficient fault tolerance, it becomes necessary for multiple robots to form teams during exploration, thereby increasing the collective observation range and reliability. Traditionally, swarm formation has often been managed by a central controller; however, from the perspectives of robustness and flexibility, it is preferable for the swarm to operate autonomously even in the absence of centralized control. In addition, the determination of exploration targets for each team is crucial for efficient exploration in such multi-team exploration scenarios. This study therefore proposes an exploration method that combines (1) an algorithm for self-organization, enabling the autonomous and dynamic formation of multiple teams, and (2) an algorithm that allows each team to autonomously determine its next exploration target (destination). In particular, for (2), this study explores a novel strategy based on large language models (LLMs), while classical frontier-based methods and deep reinforcement learning approaches have been widely studied. The effectiveness of the proposed method was validated through simulations involving tens to hundreds of robots.

</details>


### [189] [Data-Driven Control of a Magnetically Actuated Fish-Like Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04787)
*Akiyuki Koyama,Hiroaki Kawashima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出数据驱动的控制框架，结合神经网络前向动力学模型、梯度模型预测控制和模仿学习，实现磁驱动鱼形机器人的精确路径跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁驱动鱼形机器人在水下探索中具有小型化和灵活性的优势，但由于非线性流体动力学、柔性鳍迟滞以及变时长控制步长等问题，精确控制仍然是一个重大挑战。

Method: 1) 使用神经网络基于真实实验数据训练前向动力学模型，捕捉变时间步长下的状态转移；2) 将该模型集成到梯度模型预测控制架构中，优化路径跟踪的控制输入；3) 应用模仿学习近似G-MPC策略，降低实时计算成本。

Result: 仿真验证显示，G-MPC框架实现了精确的路径收敛，具有最小的均方根误差；模仿学习控制器有效复制了这种性能。

Conclusion: 该研究突出了数据驱动控制策略在微型鱼形软机器人精确导航方面的潜力，为不依赖分析建模的复杂控制问题提供了解决方案。

Abstract: Magnetically actuated fish-like robots offer promising solutions for underwater exploration due to their miniaturization and agility; however, precise control remains a significant challenge because of nonlinear fluid dynamics, flexible fin hysteresis, and the variable-duration control steps inherent to the actuation mechanism. This paper proposes a comprehensive data-driven control framework to address these complexities without relying on analytical modeling. Our methodology comprises three core components: 1) developing a forward dynamics model (FDM) using a neural network trained on real-world experimental data to capture state transitions under varying time steps; 2) integrating this FDM into a gradient-based model predictive control (G-MPC) architecture to optimize control inputs for path following; and 3) applying imitation learning to approximate the G-MPC policy, thereby reducing the computational cost for real-time implementation. We validate the approach through simulations utilizing the identified dynamics model. The results demonstrate that the G-MPC framework achieves accurate path convergence with minimal root mean square error (RMSE), and the imitation learning controller (ILC) effectively replicates this performance. This study highlights the potential of data-driven control strategies for the precise navigation of miniature, fish-like soft robots.

</details>


### [190] [On the Strengths and Weaknesses of Data for Open-set Embodied Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04819)
*Pradyumna Tambwekar,Andrew Silva,Deepak Gopinath,Jonathan DeCastro,Xiongyi Cui,Guy Rosman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究探索了在合成环境中通过多样化交互数据微调多模态基础模型，以实现对未见用户行为和任务配置的泛化能力，提出了开放集纠正辅助任务作为评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身基础模型在机器人、自动驾驶等交互式辅助场景中需要泛化到新用户和新任务，但现有方法通常假设封闭的纠正类别或依赖外部规划器，缺乏对开放集辅助能力的评估。

Method: 在Overcooked游戏中生成合成辅助数据集，微调基于LLaMA的多模态基础模型，评估模型在未见用户行为类别和新任务配置下的泛化能力，重点关注开放集纠正辅助任务。

Result: 研究表明，性能良好的模型需要覆盖多模态接地、缺陷推断和多样化场景的数据集，这些数据集的不同方面对于实现开放集辅助智能至关重要。

Conclusion: 通过合成多样化交互数据微调基础模型可以有效提升开放集辅助能力，为构建泛化性强的辅助智能系统提供了关键见解和数据要求指导。

Abstract: Embodied foundation models are increasingly performant in real-world domains such as robotics or autonomous driving. These models are often deployed in interactive or assistive settings, where it is important that these assistive models generalize to new users and new tasks. Diverse interactive data generation offers a promising avenue for providing data-efficient generalization capabilities for interactive embodied foundation models. In this paper, we investigate the generalization capabilities of a multimodal foundation model fine-tuned on diverse interactive assistance data in a synthetic domain. We explore generalization along two axes: a) assistance with unseen categories of user behavior and b) providing guidance in new configurations not encountered during training. We study a broad capability called \textbf{Open-Set Corrective Assistance}, in which the model needs to inspect lengthy user behavior and provide assistance through either corrective actions or language-based feedback. This task remains unsolved in prior work, which typically assumes closed corrective categories or relies on external planners, making it a challenging testbed for evaluating the limits of assistive data. To support this task, we generate synthetic assistive datasets in Overcooked and fine-tune a LLaMA-based model to evaluate generalization to novel tasks and user behaviors. Our approach provides key insights into the nature of assistive datasets required to enable open-set assistive intelligence. In particular, we show that performant models benefit from datasets that cover different aspects of assistance, including multimodal grounding, defect inference, and exposure to diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [191] [Task-Relevant and Irrelevant Region-Aware Augmentation for Generalizable Vision-Based Imitation Learning in Agricultural Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04845)
*Shun Hattori,Hikaru Sasaki,Takumi Hachimine,Yusuke Mizutani,Takamitsu Matsubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRAIL提出了一种区域感知的数据增强框架，通过分离任务相关和任务无关区域，分别进行知识驱动增强和随机化处理，提升农业机器人视觉模仿学习的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业机器人视觉模仿学习面临两个主要挑战：1) 演示数据稀缺；2) 视觉领域差距大，包括作物外观多样性和背景变化。现有方法泛化能力有限。

Method: DRAIL框架将视觉观察分为任务相关区域和任务无关区域。任务相关区域采用领域知识驱动的方式进行增强，保留关键视觉特征；任务无关区域则进行激进随机化，抑制虚假背景相关性。

Result: 在人工蔬菜收割和真实生菜缺陷叶片采摘任务中，DRAIL在未见过的视觉条件下相比基线方法显著提高了成功率。注意力分析和表示泛化指标表明，学习到的策略更依赖任务本质特征。

Conclusion: DRAIL通过区域感知的增强策略有效提升了农业机器人视觉模仿学习的泛化能力，使策略学习更关注任务本质特征而非偶然视觉线索，增强了鲁棒性和泛化性。

Abstract: Vision-based imitation learning has shown promise for robotic manipulation; however, its generalization remains limited in practical agricultural tasks. This limitation stems from scarce demonstration data and substantial visual domain gaps caused by i) crop-specific appearance diversity and ii) background variations. To address this limitation, we propose Dual-Region Augmentation for Imitation Learning (DRAIL), a region-aware augmentation framework designed for generalizable vision-based imitation learning in agricultural manipulation. DRAIL explicitly separates visual observations into task-relevant and task-irrelevant regions.
